# Content, attribution and modifications

The selected Wiktionnaire/Wiktionary contributor text and Lexique translations,
grammatical explanations, IPA presentation, selected-source excerpts and licensed
membership are distributed under **Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 4.0
International**. The complete applicable licence and warranty disclaimer are in
[COPYING](COPYING); its public legal text is
[CC BY-SA 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/legalcode.en).
Adaptations retain downstream CC BY-SA rights. Application/converter code has
the separate MIT licence (`LICENSE-MIT` in the prepared resource).

Credit Wiktionnaire and Wiktionary contributors, and Lexique contributors for the
recorded selection/adaptations. Every field's fixed article/version and author
history are recorded in [curated-inputs.json](curated-inputs.json), with exact
selected lines in [selected-source-excerpts.json](selected-source-excerpts.json).
Credit Boris New and Christophe Pallier for Lexique 3.83 membership/frequencies,
retaining the actual corpus contributors and source statements in
[LEXIQUE-UPSTREAM-README.txt](LEXIQUE-UPSTREAM-README.txt),
[lexique-provenance.json](lexique-provenance.json), and
[MEMBERSHIP-NOTICES.txt](MEMBERSHIP-NOTICES.txt).

Modifications by Lexique: selected one specified use per frozen
group; removed wiki markup; added selected IPA slash delimiters; translated or
adapted scoped French, English and Chinese explanations; clarified auxiliary +
past-participle construction, all pronominal forms with être and verbs requiring
être rather than avoir; retained compound-tense anteriority including future
reference times; clarified article/pronoun agreement, elision, complement and
register restrictions; distinguished the whole/entire adjective from the
distributive indefinite adjective and the adverb; retained the psychoanalytic
domain of the noun ça; and bound the four evidenced source spellings. Each field
records its own actual modification. The membership projects four accepted
columns and preserves NFC spelling, original POS, frequency values and row order.

Article-level `Import:DAF8` and other supplied source/reference notices are retained
in [selected-source-context.json](selected-source-context.json). They do not
admit unselected DAF8/TLFi bodies. The cœur/coeur contributor merger at revision
1507043 has distinct fixed article/history and older merger-context records;
credit is not reduced to the one early editor. Historical evidence identifies
authorship/source boundaries separately from current selected content. No whole
page or corpus licensing conclusion is asserted.

The elected CC BY-SA route follows [Wikimedia Terms revision 598588](https://foundation.wikimedia.org/w/index.php?title=Policy:Terms_of_Use&oldid=598588):
§7(a) contributor licensing; §7(g) reuse, attribution and imported-source notices;
§7(h) modification/addition and licensing notices. French/English contributor
policies and selected-source boundary evidence remain in the context records.
GFDL is not additionally imposed. CC BY-SA does not require the whole upstream
corpus or executable corresponding source; this core bundles its actual curated
inputs, converter, selected excerpts and notices for reproducibility.

The independently accepted auxiliary explanations express grammar facts
corroborated by [OQLF](https://vitrinelinguistique.oqlf.gouv.qc.ca/24224/la-grammaire/le-verbe/auxiliaires/emploi-des-auxiliaires-avoir-et-etre).
The adjective distinction for tout is corroborated by the
[Canadian Translation Bureau](https://nos-langues.canada.ca/fr/cles-de-la-redaction/tout-accord-de-ladjectif),
with the frozen grammatical subclasses defined by the
[Lexique 3 manual](https://www.lexique.org/databases/Lexique382/Manuel_Lexique3.html).
OQLF and Canadian Translation Bureau prose is excluded. Exact accepted texts,
usages and grammar-fact evidence are in [field-review.json](field-review.json).
The project owner's approved #81 scope includes `ça/NOM` as that batch's specialist
exception, retaining its psychoanalytic, masculine, singular and uncountability
restrictions. It is counted separately from everyday coverage. Learner acceptance
remains unrun.

## Batch 002 (#82)

The batch adds 47 independently reviewed groups and reuses `bien/ADV` and
`bien/NOM` unchanged. All new field adaptations, selected excerpts and grammatical
restrictions retain CC BY-SA 4.0, their fixed versions/spans, contributor histories
and individual modification notices. The three new spelling records use fixed
hard redirects for `t'` → `t’` and `s'` → `s’`; `s'` keeps distinct conjunction
and personal-pronoun records. They produce two additional query aliases.

Also credit **Académie française, Dictionnaire de l’Académie française,
8th edition (1932–1935)** for the declared import notices on `pouvoir`, `nous`,
`moi`, `devoir`, `oui`, `comme`, `venir` and `sur`. The independent batch review
distinguishes surviving selected wording from article-level import credit and
separately records historical revisions and the combined redistribution
obligations in [selected-source-context.json](selected-source-context.json).
Older authorship evidence does not replace the selected fixed content revision.

Frozen `où/PRO:int` corresponds to the selected source's interrogative adverb;
`où/PRO:rel` corresponds to its relative adverb. The terminology is corroborated
by [OQLF's classification of où](https://vitrinelinguistique.oqlf.gouv.qc.ca/21461/la-grammaire/les-pronoms/pronoms-relatifs/ou-employe-comme-pronom-relatif-ou-adverbe),
which also calls relative `où` a pronoun. These grammatical facts are paraphrased;
OQLF prose is excluded. The ordinary masculine, invariable letter name for
`y/NOM` is separately corroborated by
[Académie française](https://www.dictionnaire-academie.fr/article/A9Y0001);
the modern article supplies corroboration only.

Frozen row 87, `quoi/ADJ`, remains a target with four unresolved fields.
The admitted pronoun uses retain their own subclasses and do not provide
adjective content. The owner's approved annotation exception completes #82 under
its amended scope with zero admitted fields for row 87. Its original curation,
review and incomplete machine coverage records remain unchanged. Complete D2
release acceptance remains open.

## Batch 003 (#83)

The batch adds fifty independently reviewed groups and 200 fields, selecting
one use per frozen row 0101–0150. Fixed Wiktionnaire revisions before
2026-10-01 00:00 UTC supply the selected contributor text and applicable IPA;
each field records its actual English/Chinese translation or explanatory
adaptation, selected-use restriction and original contributor history.
The selected/adapted fields and excerpts retain CC BY-SA 4.0. The
[source context](selected-source-context.json) and
[independent field review](field-review.json)
document the selected spans, authorship boundaries and redistribution route.

Also credit **Académie française, Dictionnaire de l’Académie française,
8th edition (1932–1935)** for the article-level import notices on `alors`, `aussi`,
`chose`, `jamais`, `même`, `penser`, `pourquoi`, `quand`, `sur` and `très`.
The context records distinguish these explicit `Import:DAF8` notices from
`R:DAF8` reference markers on other selected pages. Surviving selected imported
wording retains its own authorship and source boundaries; article-level notices
are retained even where the selected section predates or lies outside the import.
In particular, the selected `sur/PRE` definition and contributor example predate
the DAF8 import, which added adjective material. Older revision evidence does
not replace the fixed selected revision.

The adverbial `bon` is restricted to literal `sentir bon`, using the selected
licensed phrase definition. `petit/ADV` is restricted to writing in small
characters (`écrire petit`). TLFi/Robert classifications corroborate these uses;
their prose is excluded. Frozen `pourquoi/CON` and `comment/CON` correspond to
indirect subordination by interrogative adverbs, corroborated by
[OQLF](https://vitrinelinguistique.oqlf.gouv.qc.ca/fiche-gdt/fiche/26560285/adverbe-interrogatif);
they retain the frozen subclasses without relabelling the modern lexemes as
conjunctions. Grammar-fact corroboration is paraphrased; OQLF prose is excluded.

`même/PRO:ind` uses the clean fixed `le même` definition and form table, with
applicable IPA from `même`. The Reverso-cited pronoun section is excluded.
The Alexandria citation belongs to the unselected noun section; it is not
the source of the selected adjective or adverb. The article-level Académie
notice remains in the context. Literary quotations and older XMLittré
CC BY-SA 3.0 candidates are excluded from the new fields; that licence is not
relabelled as CC BY-SA 4.0.

The owner's approved #83 scope admits `par/NOM` as a specific specialist
exception: the conventional number of strokes required to complete a single
golf hole. Its masculine noun, golf domain and standard-stroke-count restrictions
remain in the selected fields and usage. The achieved score, Georgian-letter
name and preposition are excluded. This group is accepted separately from
literal everyday coverage, alongside the existing #81 exception.

## Batches #84–#89

The resource admits 296 additional groups / 1,184 fields, preserving the original
154 groups / 616 fields and counting reused rows 360, 422 and 423 once. Fixed
Wiktionnaire and English Wiktionary contributor spans, scoped Lexique translations
and grammatical explanations retain **CC BY-SA 4.0**, full contributor histories,
individual modification notices, applicable source/import credits and the warranty
disclaimer in [COPYING](COPYING). The actual import and bibliography markers and
their version/line boundaries are retained in [selected-source-context.json](selected-source-context.json).
Credit **Académie française, Dictionnaire de l’Académie française, huitième édition
(1932–1935)** wherever the supplied DAF8 import is declared. A bibliography marker
does not grant rights to an unselected publisher's prose. Protected dictionary
definitions, quotations, media and full-page captures are excluded.

The three beau/NOM prose fields adapt **Théodore Soulice (1800–1878), Petit
dictionnaire de la langue française, à l’usage des écoles primaires**, first edition
1836, Paris: **L. Hachette / Firmin Didot frères**, printed p. 56 / scan p. 62.
The selected original noun clause is public domain. Credit Wikisource transcription
contributors through the fixed page revision **14772408**, lines **142–143**, and
its complete history, recorded in the field source/context. Selection ends before
the semicolon introducing the separate **beau idéal** clause. The original printed
author/edition and the later transcription version remain distinct. Credit Lexique
contributors for the French adaptation and English/Chinese translations, including
the grammatical explanation. Reused transcription/adapted contributions follow the
elected CC BY-SA 4.0 route; the public-domain original does not acquire a ShareAlike
obligation merely by scanning or transcription. No author endorsement is implied.

Accurate scoped mappings retain their source category: English front adjective
supplies the semantic base for French avant/ADJ, with independent French grammar
facts and French IPA; French déjà/ADV supplies the reached-state meaning for the
reactive déjà/ONO use. The lowercase ok/ADJ and ok/ADV mappings use the attested
French **OK** source form; oeil/NOM uses the explicit typographic variant **œil**.
Regional familiar point/ADV selects the negative use after the finite verb without
ne. Usito, OQLF and modern Académie material corroborate usage facts only; their
proprietary explanatory text and examples are not redistributed.

All protected adapted contributions remain under CC BY-SA 4.0, with its attribution,
modification, ShareAlike, database and no-additional-restrictions obligations.
Applicable copyright, licence/disclaimer, import and previous-modification notices
are retained. The full/Lite dictionary inputs and notices are identical; application
and converter code remain separately MIT. The frozen denominator is 4,087 groups;
zero-field annotations at rows 87 and 305 are not admitted senses. The unchanged
specialist approvals at rows 34 and 128 retain their original restrictions.

## Content batches #90–#99

The additional 494 selected senses and 1,976 FR/IPA/EN/ZH fields retain their
exact fixed source versions, selected spans, original-author boundaries and
contributor histories in the accompanying inputs. Credit Lexique contributors
for each recorded scoped adaptation, translation and construction explanation.
The original Académie française eighth collective edition (1932–1935) is
public domain under the collective-work term; subsequent Wiktionnaire
contributor transcription/adaptation and Lexique adapted contributions retain
their separate CC BY-SA 4.0 attribution and ShareAlike obligations. The original
prose is not relabelled as CC-licensed. Unselected quotations and proprietary
corroboration prose are excluded. Bibliographic/import notices are retained
separately from admitted field text.

The complete development core contains 944 admitted groups / 3,776 fields,
942 everyday groups and two approved specialist senses. Seven owner-approved
annotations retain zero content and 28 missing fields. The 4,087-group /
3,773-headword denominator, original frequencies and existing learner data
remain unchanged. Full and Lite use identical dictionary content and notices.

## Content batches #100–#109

The additional 492 selected senses / 1,968 fields retain exact fixed sources, contributor histories and selected spans.
Credit Wiktionnaire and Wikisource contributors for source transcription/adaptation and Lexique contributors for recorded scoped adaptations, explanatory translations and construction notes, under CC BY-SA 4.0. The marché replacement retains the original 1836 Théodore Soulice public-domain source credit and its fixed Wikisource transcription separately from the recorded adaptations.
Where an Académie française eighth-edition marker is recorded, retain the original 1932–1935 collective edition credit and public-domain boundary, separately from subsequent CC BY-SA 4.0 contributions. Actual original authors/import and bibliography notices are retained in selected-source-context.json. Unselected quotation bodies, media and proprietary corroboration prose are excluded.
The current development core contains 1,436 admitted groups / 5,744 fields, 1,434 everyday groups, two specialist senses and 15 approved zero-field annotations / 60 missing fields. Full and Lite use identical dictionary content and notices.

## Batches #110–#119 sources and adaptations

Frozen rows 1451–1950 add 499 selected uses and 1,996 independently reviewed fields. The existing côte/NOM record and four fields are reused unchanged. Modern meanings, pronunciation and register/construction restrictions are bound to each selected POS/use; individual adaptations and applicable historical source notices are recorded in the field and source context materials. Source spelling and capitalization bindings do not add runtime aliases.
